CDOTA_BaseNPC
	include by name: m_fFlags, m_iMaxHealth, m_lifeState, m_bTakesDamage, m_nTakeDamageFlags, m_iHealth
	exclude by name: m_bIsIllusion, m_flEncodedController, m_flPoseParameter, m_flSimulationTime, m_flexWeight, m_nForceBone, m_nHitboxSet, m_baseLayer.m_hSequence, m_vecForce, m_vecMaxs, m_vecMins, m_vecSpecifiedSurroundingMaxs, m_vecSpecifiedSurroundingMins, m_vLookTargetPosition
	exclude by user group: m_flCycle, overlay_vars
	overrides: CGameSceneNode::m_angRotation encoder qangle_pitch_yaw, CGameSceneNode::m_angRotation bitCount 8
	user group proxies: SpectatorsOnly, Abilities
	network class
	uint32 m_iUnitType
	bool m_bSelectionRingVisible
	int m_iCurrentLevel (priority: 32)
	float m_flMana (bit count: 20; min: 0; max: 65536)
